Result: 重建的自旋密度显示氟离子存在有限的磁矩，同时Mn磁化呈现各向异性，与交变磁体对称性所允许的秩5磁性多极子O₅₂（磁三十偶极子）一致。

Conclusion: 这些结果为交变磁体中的铁性多极序提供了直接的实空间证据，并确立了偏振中子衍射作为探测量子材料中隐藏磁性多极子的强大手段。

Abstract: Altermagnets are unconventional spin split magnets arising from the zero spin-orbit coupled limit. They host a magnetic multipolar order parameter yet direct real-space observation of these multipoles has remained elusive. Here we use polarized-neutron diffraction to reconstruct the three-dimensional magnetization density of the prototypical altermagnet MnF$_2$. By exploiting symmetry-selective magnetic reflections, we separate the dominant spherical Mn$^{2+}$ contribution from the much weaker anisotropic Mn magnetization and the covalent spin polarization of the fluorine ligands. The reconstructed spin density reveals a finite fluorine ion moment together with an anisotropic Mn magnetization consistent with the symmetry-allowed altermagnetic rank-5 magnetic multipole $O_{52}$(magnetic triacontadipole). These results provide direct real-space evidence of ferroic multipolar order in an altermagnet and establish polarized-neutron diffraction as a powerful probe of hidden magnetic multipoles in quantum materials.

</details>


### [101] [Electronic phase separation and emergence of a nondimerized insulating phase in VO$_2$ $(110)_{\mathit{R}}$ ultrathin films](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29116)
*S. Inoue,D. Shiga,R. Hayasaka,K. Ozawa,A. F. Santander-Syro,H. Kumigashira*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该研究通过原位光电子能谱和X射线吸收谱，系统研究了VO₂/TiO₂(110)超薄膜中电子结构和V-V二聚化的厚度依赖关系。研究发现，尽管(110)取向沿表面法线方向的降维不对V-V二聚化施加几何约束，但温度驱动的金属-绝缘体转变相关的特征谱变化仍持续到1.5 nm，而在1 nm时出现无V-V二聚化的绝缘相。这一行为与VO₂(001)R薄膜高度相似，表明降维引起的Mott不稳定性增强是VO₂超薄膜中无二聚化绝缘相产生的基本驱动力。然而，与(001)R取向不同，(110)R取向的无二聚化绝缘相与温度驱动金属-绝缘体转变相共存，其比例随厚度减小呈指数增加，在1 nm时占主导，对应的有效临界厚度约为2.2 nm。结果表明，V-V链的几何取向通过应变介导的相竞争决定了电子相分离的空间演化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解降维效应对强关联电子材料中电子结构和相变的影响，特别是VO₂超薄膜中Mott不稳定性与V-V二聚化之间的竞争关系，以及晶体取向如何调控这种竞争。

Method: 采用原位光电子能谱(photoemission spectroscopy)和X射线吸收谱(X-ray absorption spectroscopy)，对不同厚度的VO₂/TiO₂(110)超薄膜（从厚膜到1 nm）进行电子结构和V-V二聚化的系统表征，并与VO₂(001)R薄膜进行对比。

Result: (110)R取向薄膜中温度驱动Mott转变的谱学特征持续至1.5 nm；在1 nm时出现无V-V二聚化的绝缘相。该无二聚化绝缘相与Mott转变相共存，其比例随厚度减小呈指数增长并在1 nm时占主导，有效临界厚度为2.2 nm。这一行为与(001)R取向薄膜存在显著差异——后者无二聚化绝缘相不与其他相共存。

Conclusion: 降维引起的Mott不稳定性增强是VO₂超薄膜中无二聚化绝缘相出现的普适驱动力，但V-V链的几何取向通过应变介导的相竞争决定了电子相分离的空间演化方式，即(110)R取向特有的相共存行为来源于V-V链在面内的几何排布。

Abstract: Using in situ photoemission spectroscopy and x-ray absorption spectroscopy, we investigated the thickness dependence of the electronic structure and V-V dimerization in VO$_2$/TiO$_2$ (110) ultrathin films, in which the one-dimensional V-V chains along the $c_{\mathit{R}}$ axis lie in the film plane. In VO$_2$ $(110)_{\mathit{R}}$ films, the reduction in dimensionality along the surface-normal direction is not expected to impose a geometric constraint on V-V dimerization, unlike in VO$_2$ $(001)_{\mathit{R}}$ films. Nevertheless, the characteristic spectral changes associated with the temperature-driven metal-insulator transition observed in thick films persist down to 1.5 nm, whereas at 1 nm an insulating electronic phase is observed without V-V dimerization. This behavior is highly similar to that reported for VO$_2$ $(001)_{\mathit{R}}$, suggesting that the enhancement of Mott instability resulting from reduced dimensionality is a common and essential driving force for the emergence of the nondimerized insulating phase in VO$_2$ ultrathin films. Meanwhile, unlike in VO$_2$ $(001)_{\mathit{R}}$, the nondimerized insulating phase in VO$_2$ $(110)_{\mathit{R}}$ coexists with the phase exhibiting the temperature-driven metal-insulator transition. Its fraction increases exponentially with decreasing thickness and becomes dominant at 1 nm. The corresponding effective critical thickness is estimated to be 2.2 nm. These results imply that the geometric orientation of the V-V chains dictates the spatial evolution of electronic phase separation via strain-mediated phase competition.

</details>


### [102] [Evolving charge order in the CDW state of AV$_3$Sb$_5$ metals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29142)
*Anshu Kataria,Francis Pratt,Peter J. Baker,Stephen Cottrell,Miki Bonacci,Andrea Capa Salinas,Stephen D. Wilson,Zurab Guguchia,Samuele Sanna,Pietro Bonfà*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过避开交叉（ALC）μ子自旋谱学，系统研究了Cs和K基AV₃Sb₅笼目金属中的第二相变，发现其起源不能仅归因于内部磁场，而是涉及一种额外的电子机制——在电荷密度波（CDW）相内部发生的附加电荷调制，推测与RbV₃Sb₅中的现象一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AV₃Sb₅笼目金属中电子序与结构序的相互交织引发了广泛研究，但超导相之前的电子态细节仍不清楚，因此需要进一步深入理解其电子演化过程。

Method: 将此前对RbV₃Sb₅的避开交叉（ALC）μ子自旋谱学方法扩展到A=Cs和K体系，通过分析ALC谱线的变化来探测电子结构转变。

Result: 在Cs和K体系中同样识别出一个第二转变，该转变不能仅由内部磁场解释；ALC结果表明在CDW相内存在额外的电荷调制，其中Cs和Rb体系中发生在T*<T_CDW处，而K体系中则发生在接近T_CDW处。

Conclusion: AV₃Sb₅家族中普遍存在一种超越简单磁起源的第二电子转变，其表现为CDW相内的附加电荷调制，该机制在不同A位元素体系中表现出略微不同的温度位置。

Abstract: AV$_3$Sb$_5$ kagome metals are characterized by intertwined electronic and structural orders, which motivated extensive studies in recent years. Yet the details of the electronic state preceding the superconducting phase remain poorly understood. Here we extend our previous investigation [Phys. Rev. Research 7, L032046 (2025)] of RbV$_3$Sb$_5$ using avoided level crossing (ALC) muon-spin spectroscopy to the $A$ = Cs and K systems. Consistent with our previous study, we identify a second transition whose origin cannot be attributed solely to an internal magnetic field, indicating the involvement of an additional electronic mechanism that subtly modifies the charge distribution within the V plane. In particular, the ALC results point towards an additional charge modulation taking place within the charge density wave (CDW) phase and occurring at $T^{*}<T_{CDW}$ for $A$ = Cs and Rb, or in the vicinity of $T_{CDW}$ for $A$ = K.

</details>


### [103] [Crystal electric field excitations and an effective-spin-$1/2$ ground doublet in the hyperkagome magnet Yb$_3$Sc$_2$Ga$_3$O$_{12}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29276)
*Tingjun Zhang,Zehao Wang,Douglas L. Abernathy,Rong-Zhu Lin,Steven J. Gomez Alvarado,Chien-Lung Huang,Pengcheng Dai*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过非弹性中子散射（INS）实验测定了稀士超Kagome磁性材料Yb₃Sc₂Ga₃O₁₂中Yb³⁺离子的晶场（CEF）激发谱，观测到约58、68和74 meV三个近乎无色散的磁性激发，对应J=7/2多重态中基态Kramers双峰向三个激发双峰的跃迁。Stevens算子分析在局域D₂对称性约束下成功再现了激发能量和谱权重，确定了Ising型基态g张量，并建立了良好隔离的J_eff=1/2低能自由度。该结果修正了此前由体测量推断的能级方案，为构建有效交换哈密顿量奠定了基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稀土超Kagome磁体Yb₃Sc₂Ga₃O₁₂由于其几何阻挫结构可能蕴藏新颖量子磁性现象，但此前对其单离子性质的理解存在不确定性——从体测量推断的晶场能级方案缺乏直接实验验证。确定精确的晶场能级和基态波函数是构建有效交换模型、理解低温集体激发的必要前提。

Method: 采用非弹性中子散射（INS）技术直接测量Yb³⁺的晶场激发谱，同时利用Stevens算子分析在局域222（D₂）对称性约束下拟合激发能量与谱权重。采用两种独立的拟合协议进行交叉验证，以避免晶场参数的非唯一性问题，并通过计算磁化率、磁化强度及比热中Schottky异常的场依赖行为对单离子模型进行多物理量验证。

Result: 观测到三个近乎无色散的磁性激发（58、68、74 meV），确认了基态Kramers双峰与三个激发双峰之间的跃迁。两种独立拟合协议给出一致的激发能量、基态波函数和g张量，确定了Ising型基态特征。第一激发双峰距基态约58 meV，远大于低温集体磁性所涉及的能量尺度，从而确立了良好隔离的有效J_eff=1/2自由度。直接测量的晶场谱显著修正了此前体测量推断的能级方案，但保留了低温赝自旋描述的本质。单离子模型还能复现磁化率、磁化强度及Schottky异常的场演化特征。

Conclusion: 该工作通过直接的INS测量建立了Yb₃Sc₂Ga₃O₁₂中Yb³⁺单离子晶场模型的微观基础，为构建有效交换哈密顿量提供了可靠的输入参数，也为解释未来低能集体激发测量建立了必要的单离子参照系。同时展示了INS晶场测量在修正体测量推断方案、但保留有效低能描述方面的优势与稳健性。

Abstract: We use inelastic neutron scattering (INS) to determine the crystal electric field (CEF) excitations of Yb$^{3+}$ in the rare-earth hyperkagome magnet Yb$_3$Sc$_2$Ga$_3$O$_{12}$. Three nearly dispersionless magnetic excitations are observed near 58, 68, and 74~meV, corresponding to transitions from the ground-state Kramers doublet to the three excited doublets of the $J=7/2$ multiplet. A Stevens-operator analysis constrained by the local $222$ ($D_2$) symmetry reproduces the excitation energies and spectral weights and yields an Ising-type ground-state $g$ tensor. The first excited doublet lies approximately 58~meV above the ground state, establishing a well-isolated effective $J_{\mathrm{eff}}=1/2$ degree of freedom over the low-temperature regime relevant to collective magnetism. Notably, the directly measured CEF spectrum substantially revises the level scheme previously inferred from bulk measurements, while preserving the essential low-energy pseudospin description. Two independent fitting protocols give consistent excitation energies, ground-doublet wave functions, and $g$ tensors, despite the nonuniqueness of the individual CEF parameters. The resulting single-ion model also reproduces the characteristic susceptibility, magnetization, and field evolution of the Schottky anomaly in specific heat. These results establish the microscopic single-ion basis needed to construct an effective exchange Hamiltonian and to interpret future measurements of low-energy collective excitations in Yb$_3$Sc$_2$Ga$_3$O$_{12}$.

</details>


### [104] [Experimental observation of decoupled spin subsystems in decorated square kagomé lattice magnets of the nabokoite family](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29323)
*V. N. Glazkov,Ya V. Rebrov,M. A. Dubovitskii,M. M. Markina,A. F. Murtazoev,P. S. Berdonosov,A. N. Vasiliev*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 通过电子顺磁共振（EPR）绝对校准实验，发现Nabokoite族化合物中的复杂交换网络会分裂为两个几乎解耦的自旋子体系：仅部分铜自旋具有EPR活性并在Néel温度点发生磁有序，而EPR沉默的自旋子体系对总磁化率的贡献随降温而减小，体现了同一化合物中磁有序与可能的自旋液体动力学共存。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 方形kagomé晶格（SKL）是研究二维几何阻挫的理想平台，而Nabokoite族化合物将其扩展到三维网络结构（2D SKL层间插有层间自旋）。研究者希望通过实验手段揭示这种装饰化阻挫晶格中复杂的交换网络行为，挑战传统认为阻挫晶格中交换网络统一作用的模型。

Method: 使用电子顺磁共振（EPR）技术，对Nabokoite族化合物Nabok{A}{X}（A=Na, K, Cs, Rb；X=Cl, Br）进行EPR吸收的绝对校准测量，并将EPR吸收信号与静态磁化率进行对比分析，以确定各自旋子体系对总磁响应的贡献及其温度依赖行为。

Result: 研究发现EPR吸收的绝对校准显示只有一部分铜自旋具有EPR活性，且这部分自旋在Néel温度点发生磁有序。同时，EPR吸收与静态磁化率的对比表明，EPR沉默的自旋子体系对总磁化率的贡献随冷却而减小，证明该化合物中存在两个几乎完全解耦的自旋子体系。

Conclusion: 该研究直接观察到磁有序与可能存在的自旋液体动力学在同一化合物中共存的实验证据，这对装饰化阻挫晶格中统一交换网络的传统观念提出了挑战，为理解复杂阻挫体系的物理本质提供了新的视角。

Abstract: The square kagomé lattice (SKL) offers a model platform for investigating geometric frustration in 2D systems. Nabokoite-family compounds \nabok{A}{X} (A=Na, K, Cs, Rb and X=Cl, Br) extend this physics to a 3D network, where 2D SKL layers are decorated by interlayer spins. Using electron paramagnetic resonance (EPR), we demonstrate a dramatic splitting of this complex exchange network into two virtually decoupled spin subsystems: absolute calibration of the electron paramagnetic resonance (EPR) absorption reveals that only a fraction of all copper spins in nabokoites is EPR-active and this fraction of the spins orders at the Néel point. Comparison of the EPR absorption and static susceptibility indicates that contribution of the EPR-silent spin subsystem to total magnetic susceptibility decreases on cooling. This direct observation of coexisting magnetic order and possible spin-liquid dynamics within a single compound challenges conventional models of unified exchange networks in decorated frustrated lattices.

</details>


### [105] [Collinear and noncollinear antiferromagnetic ordering in a highly frustrated decorated square kagomé lattice antiferromagnets of the nabokoite family](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29330)
*V. N. Glazkov,Ya V. Rebrov,M. A. Dubovitskii,M. M. Markina,K. V. Zakharov,A. F. Murtazoev,P. S. Berdonosov,A. N. Vasiliev*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过多频电子自旋共振和热力学测量（比热、磁化率、介电常数）系统研究了Nabokoite家族化合物ACu7(TeO4)(SO4)5X的磁有序行为，发现轻碱金属离子（K、Na）与重碱金属离子（Rb、Cs）化合物表现出本质不同的基态选择：重碱金属体系呈现常规共线反铁磁序，而轻碱金属体系则通过铁电相变解除阻挫并经历两步相变形成非常规非共线磁序。有限尺寸团簇建模表明交换耦合参数位于不同量子基态的临界边界附近是这种差异的关键。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Nabokoite家族化合物包含受挫的二维方形kagomé晶格层以及额外的层间磁性离子，是研究几何阻挫体系中磁序调控的理想平台。理解不同碱金属离子对低温磁基态选择的影响机制，有助于揭示阻挫体系中经典磁序与非共线磁序之间的竞争规律。

Method: 采用多频电子自旋共振（ESR）光谱与热力学测量（比热、磁化率、介电常数）相结合的手段，系统表征Nabokoite家族化合物的磁有序行为。同时采用有限尺寸团簇模型对Nabokoite结构的金字塔形结构块进行量子基态分析，研究交换耦合参数的临界邻近性。

Result: 重碱金属离子（Rb、Cs）Nabokoite化合物以易轴各向异性形成常规共线反铁磁有序，参数在全子族内非常接近。轻碱金属离子（K、Na）化合物则先在25-90K发生铁电相变解除阻挫，随后通过两步相变（Tc1约5-6K，Tc2约3-4K）形成非共线磁序，并由非Larmor反铁磁共振模式证实。轻碱金属体系具有三个零场磁振子能隙和两个自旋重取向场，且不同化合物间数值差异显著。

Conclusion: 轻碱与重碱金属离子Nabokoite化合物在低温磁基态上存在质的差异，其根源可能在于Nabokoite结构金字塔结构块的交换耦合参数恰好位于不同量子基态的分界线附近，这一临界邻近性决定了体系走向完全不同的有序态。

Abstract: Nabokoite family compounds ACu$_7$(TeO$_4$)(SO4)$_5$X (A=Na, K, Rb, Cs; X=Cl, Br) host frustrated 2D square kagom'{e} lattice layers decorated by additional inter-layer magnetic ions. We study magnetic order in nabokoites with multi-frequency electron spin resonance spectroscopy and thermodynamic measurement (specific heat, magnetization and dielectric permittivity). Our study reveals that the choice of the low-temperature ground state is qualitatively different in light-alkali-ion (K, Na) and heavy-alkali-ion (Rb, Cs) compounds. Heavy-alkali-ion nabokoites order in conventional collinear antiferromagnetic pattern with easy-axis anisotropy. The parameters of the ordered antiferromagnetic state are very close for all heavy-alkali-ion subfamily. Light-alkali-ion members of nabokoite family demonstrate much more complicated route to the ordered state: firstly, a ferroelectric transition at 25-90K lifts the frustration and thus pre-cooks the low-temperature ordering; secondly, an unusual noncollinear magnetic order develops via two-step phase transition with first transition temperature $T_{c1}\simeq 5-6$K and the second transition at $T_{c2}\simeq 3-4$K. Noncollinear order is evidenced by observation of characteristic non-Larmor antiferromagnetic resonance mode. Spin dynamics of light-alkali-ion nabokoites is characterized by three zero-field magnon gaps and two spin-reorientation fields, the values of magnon gaps and critical fields are quite different for different compounds. The finite-size cluster modeling of pyramidal structural block of nabokoite structure combined suggests that the critical closeness of the nabokoite exchange coupling parameters to the border-line between the different quantum ground state of pyramidal building block of nabokoite structure could be the clue to the choice of qualitatively different ordered state in light- and heavy-alkali-ion nabokoites.

</details>


### [106] [Magnetic Field-Tunable Repulsive Exciton-Exciton Interaction in the van der Waals Antiferromagnet NiPS$_3$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29701)
*Kaiyang Huang,Jaena Park,Zhuo Yang,Je-Geun Park,Atsuhiko Miyata,Yoshimitsu Kohama,Yasuhiro H. Matsuda*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过高达178 T的脉冲磁场磁光实验，系统研究了二维范德华晶体NiPS₃中两个超窄激子吸收峰（约1.5 eV）在高磁场下的行为，观察到两峰之间显著的互斥效应与振子强度再分布，并构建了最小二能级相互作用模型进行半定量解释，揭示了磁场可调的激子-激子耦合是该材料的高场主导响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究者希望探究超强磁场是否会改变NiPS₃中两个超窄吸收峰之间由相互作用驱动的杂化。此前已广泛认为这两个峰源于自旋-轨道纠缠的三重态到单重态的跃迁，且表现出显著的依赖于自旋的特性，但其在高场下的相互作用响应尚不清楚。

Method: 作者对NiPS₃进行了脉冲磁场下（最高178 T）的系统性磁光测量，捕捉两个尖锐激子峰的演化。同时构建了一个最小化的二能级相互作用模型，将实验数据与模型进行半定量对比分析。

Result: 在高磁场下观察到两个激子峰之间明显的相互排斥（mutual repulsion），伴随着振子强度的重新分配；而带边在实验分辨率内未检测到可感知的场致位移。二能级相互作用模型与实验数据半定量吻合，确认了磁场可调的激子-激子耦合主导NiPS₃的高场响应。

Conclusion: 该工作揭示了NiPS₃中激子-激子耦合可由磁场调控这一关键物理特征，并将其确立为一个研究磁性范德华绝缘体中强关联激子物理的新型实验平台。

Abstract: Two ultra-narrow absorption peaks around 1.5 eV, which are widely believed to originate from a transition from a spin-orbital entangled triplet to a singlet state, in the two-dimensional van der Waals crystal NiPS$_3$, have attracted considerable attention because of their pronounced spin-dependent character. An interesting question is whether ultrahigh magnetic fields modify an interaction-driven hybridization between those two peaks. In this work we perform systematic magneto-optical measurements of NiPS$_3$ in pulsed magnetic fields of up to 178 T and observe a pronounced mutual repulsion between the two sharp exciton peaks accompanied by a redistribution of oscillator strength, while the band edge shows no detectable field-induced shift within our experimental resolution. We construct a minimal two-level interaction model and compare it semi-quantitatively with the experimental data. Our results reveal a magnetic-field-tunable exciton-exciton coupling as the dominant high-field response of NiPS$_3$, and clarify this material as a new experimental platform for exploring strongly correlated exciton physics in magnetic van der Waals insulators.

</details>


### [107] [Emergent Pair Density Wave and Incoherent Metallic State in a Strongly Correlated Doped System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29742)
*Soham Maiti,Nandan Pakhira,A. Taraphder*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文在slave-rotor平均场框架下研究反铁磁（AFM）与d波超导（SC）相互作用的动力学涌现态，发现帕里科夫-德热纳-武尔夫（PDW）序会在两者共存区动态涌现，且强关联效应显著改变AFM-SC共存相图并系统移动四临界点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解强关联电子系统中反铁磁序与d波超导序的微观共存机制及其相互作用，特别是这些竞争有序态是否能够动态地涌现出新的量子态（如PDW），从而揭示铜氧化物高温超导体等强关联材料中的复杂相图。

Method: 采用slave-rotor平均场方法，将电子分解为自旋（fermionic）和电荷（bosonic rotor）两个自由度，从而在平均场框架内捕获超出传统方法的强关联效应。通过求解AFM与SC序参量、转子凝聚振幅φ以及PDW序参量的自洽方程，系统分析掺杂、温度以及关联强度对相图的影响。

Result: （1）PDW序在AFM与SC共存区域动态涌现；（2）强关联显著修改AFM-SC共存区并使四临界点系统性移动；（3）转子凝聚振幅φ表征电荷相干性，φ≠0区域显示相干准粒子带与不相干Hubbard-like激发共存，而φ=0区域仅存不相干谱权重；（4）SC相具有与d波配对一致的节准粒子。

Conclusion: AFM与SC的相互作用可在强关联条件下动力学地产生PDW态，且关联强度主导了从相干金属到不相干金属的交叉行为，这对理解铜氧化物高温超导体的复杂相图具有重要意义。

Abstract: We investigate the dynamical emergence of a state driven by the interplay between antiferromagnetism (AFM) and singlet $d$-wave superconductivity (SC) within a slave-rotor mean-field framework. By decomposing the electron into charge and spin degrees of freedom, the formalism captures strong-correlation effects beyond conventional mean-field approaches. A PDW order is found to emerge dynamically in the coexistence region of AFM and SC. The AFM-SC coexistence region is significantly modified with correlation, leading to a systematic shift of the tetra-critical point. The doping and temperature evolution of the AFM, SC, and PDW order parameters, together with the rotor condensate amplitude $φ$, which characterizes charge coherence, shows a crossover from a coherent to an incoherent metal with the suppression of coherent quasi-particle spectral weight. Moreover, in the AFM + $(φ\neq 0)$ region, coherent quasi-particle bands coexist with incoherent Hubbard-like excitations, whereas only incoherent spectral features survive in the AFM + $(φ= 0)$ regime. The SC phase exhibits nodal quasi-particles consistent with $d$-wave pairing.

</details>


### [108] [Thermal Hall Signatures of Distinct Schwinger-Boson Flux Sectors on the Honeycomb Lattice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29792)
*Daiki Sasamoto*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过有限温度Schwinger-boson平均场理论与Kubo公式，比较了受挫J1-J2海森堡模型中0-通量、π-通量和手性π/2-通量三个部分的子平均场鞍点解下的自旋子热霍尔电导率，发现其符号和温度依赖可作为区分不同通量结构（部分子平均场）的输运指纹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 热输运是探测受挫磁体中电荷中性激发的体探针，但尚不清楚它能否区分同一自旋系统下不同的部分子平均场结构。该问题在蜂窝格点上尤为相关，因为存在0-通量、π-通量和手性π/2-通量三种不同扇区，需要一种输运手段来区分它们。

Method: 采用有限温度Schwinger-boson平均场理论和适用于玻色子Bogoliubov-de Gennes系统的Kubo公式，在包含最近邻交换、次近邻Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya相互作用及垂直磁场的J1-J2海森堡模型中，对三个通量扇区进行自洽鞍点求解，并计算内禀自旋子热霍尔电导率κxy/T。

Result: 在三个分支均有能隙的共同参数点处：0-通量响应为正，π-通量响应为负（在D>0、h>0及本文符号约定下）；π/2-通量响应随温度升高发生符号翻转。此外，六重旋转与时间反演组合的投影对称性使零场下固定手性π/2-通量畴的响应为零。

Conclusion: 在平均场框架内，κxy/T的符号和温度依赖可作为对通量敏感的输运特征，用于区分蜂窝格点受挫磁体中的不同部分子平均场（通量）结构。

Abstract: Thermal transport offers a bulk probe of charge-neutral excitations in frustrated magnets. It remains challenging, however, to determine whether such transport can distinguish different parton mean-field structures of the same spin system. This question is particularly relevant on the honeycomb lattice, where distinct $0$-flux, $π$-flux, and chiral $π/2$-flux sectors have been proposed. In this paper, we compare self-consistent saddle-point solutions in these three sectors for a frustrated $J_1-J_2$ Heisenberg model with a next-nearest-neighbor Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya interaction and a perpendicular magnetic field. Using finite-temperature Schwinger-boson mean-field theory and the Kubo formula for bosonic Bogoliubov-de Gennes systems, we evaluate the intrinsic spinon thermal Hall conductivity. At a common parameter point where all three branches remain gapped, the $0$-flux response is positive and the $π$-flux response is negative for $D>0$ and $h>0$ under the sign conventions used in this paper, whereas the $π/2$-flux response changes sign with increasing temperature. We further show that a projective symmetry combining a sixfold rotation with time reversal forces the zero-field response of a fixed chiral $π/2$-flux domain to vanish. Within the mean-field regime examined in this paper, the sign and temperature dependence of $κ_{xy}/T$ therefore provide a flux-sensitive transport signature.

</details>


### [109] [Symmetry-Enforced Topological Structures in Quantum Phase Diagrams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29915)
*Linhao Li,Yuan Yao*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究有能隙系统中量子相图的拓扑结构，通过识别相图中不可收缩的环（即S^1-族）来刻画基态简并性中的非平凡结构。作者揭示了对称性在该分类中的关键角色，特别是三种特殊的对称性交互作用：混合反常、半直积对称性关系以及非可逆算子对称性，这些结构导致了传统"独立对称性"分类无法触及的新型S^1-族分类。文章还给出了这些S^1-族的晶格实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有的量子相图拓扑分类方法主要基于"独立"对称性的假设，无法捕捉对称性之间复杂交互作用（如混合反常、半直积关系、非可逆对称性）所诱导的相图拓扑结构。作者旨在填补这一空白，系统研究对称性交互如何丰富S^1-族的分类，从而更完整地刻画有能隙系统的量子相图拓扑。

Method: 作者通过识别有能隙相图内不可收缩的S^1环来研究相图拓扑结构，系统地分析了三种对称性交互机制：(i)非平凡混合反常，(ii)自发破缺与未破缺对称性之间的半直积关系，以及(iii)含非可逆算子的对称性。在此基础上，构造了这些S^1-族的晶格模型实现，并显式展示了其新的代数结构。

Result: 研究发现这几种对称性交互作用能够产生传统基于独立对称性的分类方法所无法得到的新型S^1-族分类。作者成功构造了相应晶格模型，明确展示了这些S^1-族中非平凡的代数结构。

Conclusion: 对称性之间的交互作用（而非仅独立对称性）在量子相图的拓扑分类中扮演着关键角色。混合反常、半直积关系和非可逆对称性可以产生全新的S^1-族分类，扩展了已有分类框架，为理解有能隙多体系统的量子相图提供了更全面的拓扑视角。

Abstract: We study the topological structure of the quantum phase diagram of gapped systems by identifying the noncontractibility of loops, so-called $S^1$-families, within the gapped phase diagram in which many-body Hamiltonians can have nontrivial ground-state degeneracy. We manifest the role of symmetries in such $S^1$-family classifications by the exotic symmetry interplay: (i) nontrivial mixed anomalies, (ii) semi-direct product relation between the spontaneously broken and the unbroken symmetries, and (iii) symmetry with noninvertible operators. We find that such structures lead to the novel $S^1$-family classifications inaccessible by earlier classifications based on ``independent'' symmetries. Furthermore, we construct lattice realizations of these $S^1$-families and explicitly demonstrate their novel algebraic structures.

</details>


### [110] [Holographic Representations of Topological Quantum Criticality: Emergent Symmetry Approach around the Bott Clock](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30335)
*Fan Yang,Fei Zhou*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文利用涌现对称性的思想，为玻特时钟中费米子类的一系列拓扑量子临界点（tQCP）构造全息表示，通过将相邻拓扑类自由度加倍来构建(d+1)维晶格模型，其边界表现出与原始d维tQCP相同的红外动力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓扑量子临界点（tQCP）是连接不同对称性保护拓扑相之间的量子临界点，理解其全息/体边界对应关系是凝聚态物理的重要问题。本文希望利用涌现对称性来构造这些tQCP的更高维全息表示，从而更深入地理解其低能动力学与拓扑结构之间的联系。

Method: 作者采用以下方法：（1）证明在玻特时钟中，受保护对称性G_p保护的d维不同拓扑相的tQCP处存在涌现对称性U_EM；（2）利用该涌现对称性，通过将对称性升级为U_EM×G_p或U_EM⋉G_p等，构造(d+1)维晶格模型；（3）通过将d维相邻拓扑类的自由度加倍，构建具有期望扩大对称群的高维晶格模型，使其恰好属于玻特时钟上逆时针方向的相邻拓扑类；（4）研究这些(d+1)维模型的d维边界，展示其红外行为与原始d维tQCP一致。

Result: 成功证明了玻特时钟中tQCP处涌现对称性U_EM的存在，并利用该对称性系统构造了(d+1)维全息晶格模型。这些模型的对称群精确属于玻特时钟上逆时针方向的相邻拓扑类，且其d维边界具有与原始tQCP相同的红外动力学，尽管其对称性较低。

Conclusion: 这项工作建立了拓扑量子临界点与高维拓扑相之间的全息对应关系，表明tQCP可以由更高维的体态拓扑相通过边界实现来表征，为理解量子临界现象中的涌现对称性和体边界对应原理提供了新的框架。

Abstract: In this article, we apply the idea of emergent symmetries to construct holographic representations of a broad class of topological quantum critical points (tQCPs) that appear in fermionic classes in the Bott clock. We show explicitly that around the Bott clock, an emergent symmetry $U_{EM}$ exists at a tQCP between different symmetry protected gapped phases with protecting symmetry $G_p$ in $d$ spatial dimensions. This emergent symmetry can be used to construct a $(d+1)$ spatial dimensional lattice model with a properly upgraded symmetry such as $U_{EM}\times G_p$ or $U_{EM} \rtimes G_p$, etc. By doubling the degrees of freedom of the adjacent topological class in $d$-dimensions, we successfully show that the $(d+1)$-dimensional lattice models constructed in this way have the desired enlarged symmetry groups that precisely belong to the adjacent topological classes, counterclockwise around the Bott clock. Furthermore, $d$-dimensional boundaries of these $(d+1)$ dimensional lattice models of gapped topological classes are shown to exhibit identical infrared dynamics as the corresponding tQCPs in the $d$-dimensional adjacent topological phases in the Bott clock, with lower symmetries.

</details>


### [111] [Pseudospin Dynamics of Charge Order](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30381)
*Ping Tang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文从扩展Hubbard模型出发，将电荷有序态映射为交错的赝自旋序，推导出类似于双层反铁磁体的Landau-Lifshitz-Gilbert方程，并证明外电场可直接控制赝自旋序，超过阈值电场可驱动电荷序极性的相干反转。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电荷有序通常被视为电子密度的静态调幅，尽管实验上已具备探测和操控其非平衡演化的能力，但相较于自旋有序系统，电荷序动力学及其外部驱动控制的微观理论框架仍很不完善。

Method: 从扩展Hubbard模型出发，推导出一个有效的赝自旋模型，将电荷有序态映射为交错赝自旋序；电荷序动力学由赝自旋的Landau-Lifshitz-Gilbert型方程描述，与双层反铁磁体的方程紧密类似。

Result: 证明外电场直接控制交错赝自旋序，在超过阈值电场时，通过失稳集体赝自旋激发，驱动电荷序极性的相干反转。

Conclusion: 建立了电荷赝自旋作为电荷序相干切换与控制的微观动力学自由度，提供了自旋电子学中自旋序动力学框架在电荷扇区的对应物。

Abstract: Charge order is conventionally viewed as a static modulation of the electronic density, despite growing experimental capabilities to probe and manipulate its nonequilibrium evolution. In contrast to spin-ordered systems, a microscopic framework for charge-order dynamics and its control under external driving remains largely underdeveloped. Here, starting from an extended Hubbard model, we derive an effective pseudospin model in which the charge-ordered state maps onto staggered pseudospin order. The resulting charge-order dynamics is governed by Landau--Lifshitz--Gilbert-like equations for the pseudospins, closely analogous to those of a bipartite antiferromagnet. We show that an external electric field directly controls the staggered pseudospin order and, above a threshold field, drives coherent reversal of the charge-order polarity by destabilizing collective pseudospin excitations. Our results establish the charge pseudospin as a microscopic dynamical degree of freedom for coherent switching and control of charge order, providing a charge-sector analogue of the well-established framework for spin-order dynamics in spintronics.

</details>


### [112] [Chiral Color Ice: Exact Local Handedness Constraints and Möbius Zero Modes in Frustrated Magnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30802)
*Péter Kránitz,Yasir Iqbal,Karlo Penc*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文构造了SU(2)不变的局域手性约束哈密顿量，其零能空间由特定手性符号的四面体单态构成。通过B†B因子化形式，该约束在相干态极限下转化为Möbius变换决定的非线性约束，并在不同晶格连通性下产生迥异的量子集体行为：角共享晶格保持指数级简并的量子基态核，边共享晶格支持低维零模，三角覆盖则在零能处包含完整的四面体磁有序Anderson塔。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统冰规则型局域约束只约束通量类量，对手性（handedness）不敏感。作者希望展示手性本身可以被精确地施加为一个局域量子约束，且不选择自旋空间中的特定轴。这为理解局域手性、非线性约束几何与量子简并之间的关系提供了一个可处理的框架。

Method: 构造正半定、SU(2)不变的父哈密顿量。对于自旋1/2，局域项为秩1投影算子，投影到手性四面体单态；对任意自旋，局域项因子化为B†B，其中B为单态湮灭算子。通过相干态分析，零能条件等价于一个与S无关的非线性约束：三个自旋方向通过Möbius变换确定第四个。该变换的复合定义约束幺正性（holonomy），并与不同晶格连通性（角共享、边共享、三角覆盖）结合产生不同零能模式。

Result: 角共享晶格（如烧绿石型）具有指数级大的量子基态核，且其严格下界超过常规冰基准；边共享晶格支持次维度平面或线零模；三角构造抑制非均匀相干形变，并在恰好零能处包含完整的四面体磁有序Anderson塔。两个不等价三角覆盖显示出简谐零模计数不决定量子核的大小。

Conclusion: 本文建立了局域手性约束与量子简并之间的联系，表明约束网络的连通性直接决定了基态简并的结构和规模。该框架将手性、非线性约束几何和量子简并三者解耦，为受挫磁性中手性约束的研究提供了新的理论工具。

Abstract: Local constraints govern the low-energy physics of frustrated matter, but familiar ice-type rules constrain flux-like quantities and are insensitive to handedness. Here we show that handedness itself can be imposed as an exact local quantum constraint without selecting an axis in spin space. We construct positive-semidefinite, SU(2)-invariant parent Hamiltonians whose complete zero-energy space on a tetrahedron has a prescribed chirality sign, rather than selecting a particular chiral wave function. For spin-1/2 the local term is a rank-one projector onto a chiral tetrahedral singlet, while for arbitrary spin it factorizes as $B^\dagger B$ through a singlet-annihilation operator, with a completely characterized kernel given by the span of the globally rotated chiral color-ice states. For coherent states, the same zero-energy condition becomes an $S$-independent nonlinear constraint in which three spin directions determine the fourth through a Möbius transformation; compositions of these maps define constraint holonomies on extended lattices. Connecting the same local constraint in different ways produces qualitatively different collective regimes: corner-sharing lattices retain exponentially large quantum ground-state kernels, with rigorous lower bounds exceeding conventional ice benchmarks; edge-sharing lattices support subdimensional plane or line zero modes; while triangular constructions suppress nonuniform coherent deformations and contain the complete Anderson tower of tetrahedral magnetic order at exactly zero energy. Two inequivalent triangular coverings further show that harmonic zero-mode counting does not determine the size of the quantum kernel. These results establish a tractable setting in which local handedness, nonlinear constraint geometry, and quantum degeneracy can be disentangled and related directly to the connectivity of the constraint network.

</details>

### [113] [Light-induced nonconservative static forces in many-body systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29122)
*Jinze Li,Xuemei Yang,Zhiyuan Sun*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究量子多体系统中周期性驱动（光场）对电子有序参量产生的静态力，揭示了由耗散（即光吸收）导致的非保守力成分，并分别在激子绝缘体和不相称电荷密度波材料中展示了该效应产生的可观测物理后果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 周期性驱动在量子多体系统中常能对慢集体自由度产生有效的静态力，但以往研究多聚焦于保守力。作者旨在揭示由耗散（光吸收）诱导的非保守力成分，这是一个此前未被深入认识的重要物理机制。

Method: 通过理论分析证明光场对电子有序参量产生的静态力可包含源于耗散（光学吸收）的非保守分量，并在两个具体物理体系中加以验证：(1) 激子绝缘体中带间激发对激子有序参量相位产生非保守力；(2) 不相称电荷密度波材料中传播光场对有序参量相位产生非保守力。

Result: 在激子绝缘体中，非保守力导致激子有序参量相位加速，表现为激子凝聚体光子发射峰的红移/蓝移。在不相称电荷密度波材料中，该非保守力驱动电荷密度波进入滑动运动，通过拓扑Thouless泵浦产生直流电流。

Conclusion: 耗散（光吸收）可以为光场对有序参量的静态力引入非保守分量，这一效应在凝聚态系统中具有可观察的物理后果，为光驱动量子材料中的有序参量操控提供了新机制。

Abstract: In a quantum many-body system, a periodic drive can often generate effective static forces on the slow collective degrees of freedom. We study the static forces generated by light on electronic order parameters in solid-state systems. We show that the forces can have nonconservative components that originate from dissipation, i.e., optical absorption. This effect is demonstrated in two nontrivial examples. In excitonic insulators, via interband excitations, the light field generates a nonconservative force on the phase of the excitonic order parameter. This force leads to an acceleration of the phase, which manifests as a shift of the photon emission peak from an exciton condensate. In materials with an incommensurate charge density wave, a propagating light field generates a nonconservative force on the phase of its order parameter. It drives the charge density wave into sliding motion, leading to a DC electric current via topological Thouless pumping.

</details>


### [114] [Ornstein-Uhlenbeck Process Driven by Multiple Dichotomous Noises](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29226)
*Silvio Kalaj,Dongho Lee,Enzo Marinari,Jae-Hyung Jeon,Pascal Viot,Gleb Oshanin*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究由K个独立二值（dichotomous）噪声叠加驱动的广义Ornstein-Uhlenbeck过程，获得了平稳位置分布和所有累积量的精确表达式，揭示了包括紧支撑、代数支点奇异性、边界发散和多重极值在内的丰富结构，并通过映射到异质随机飞行过程给出概率解释，同时分析了多个极限以及无序平均情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的Ornstein-Uhlenbeck过程由平衡态高斯白噪声驱动，然而许多实际系统面临的是具有有限相关时间和有界幅值的非平衡涨落。理解这类由二值噪声叠加驱动的广义过程是否仍具有与经典情形类似的属性，以及其平稳态的非平凡结构，是本文的基本动机。此外，随机异质（无序）对系统统计性质的影响也鲜有研究。

Method: 作者对由K个具有任意固定幅值和切换率的独立二值噪声之和驱动的广义Ornstein-Uhlenbeck过程进行精确求解。方法包括：推导平稳位置分布和所有累积量的精确闭合表达式；建立与具有有界跳跃的异质随机飞行过程的映射以获得概率论解释；分析多个极限情形（如大量噪声源的Gauss统计交叉）；对具有指数分布淬火幅值的系综，利用无序平均技术精确求解平均后的平稳分布。

Result: 研究发现平稳态具有出乎意料的丰富结构：分布具有紧支撑、代数支点奇异性、边界发散以及多重极值。建立了与异质随机飞行过程的映射，给出平稳测度的透明概率解释。在大量噪声源极限下恢复Gauss统计。对于指数分布的淬火无序幅值，无序平均后的平稳分布呈现由非平凡指数代数因子修饰的指数尾部，表明无序从根本上改变了平稳态的结构。

Conclusion: 这项工作表明，由多源二值噪声驱动的广义Ornstein-Uhlenbeck过程即使在无噪声源数量较大的情况下也超越经典Gauss行为，其平稳态具有丰富的非平凡特征。无序的引入进一步改变了平稳态，产生指数尾部的代数修饰。这些结果为理解受限非平衡涨落驱动系统的统计性质提供了精确且普适的理论框架。

Abstract: We study a generalized Ornstein-Uhlenbeck process driven by a superposition of $K$ independent dichotomous noises with arbitrary fixed amplitudes and switching rates. Unlike the classical Ornstein-Uhlenbeck process driven by equilibrium Gaussian white noise, the present system is governed by bounded nonequilibrium fluctuations with finite correlation times. We obtain exact expressions for the stationary position distribution and all cumulants, and show that the stationary state possesses an unexpectedly rich structure, including compact support, algebraic branch-point singularities, edge divergences, and multiple extrema. We establish a mapping onto a heterogeneous random-flight process with bounded jumps, yielding a transparent probabilistic interpretation of the stationary measure. We further analyze several limiting regimes, including the crossover to Gaussian statistics for large numbers of noise sources. For ensembles with exponentially-distributed quenched amplitudes, we derive exact disorder-averaged stationary distributions and show that disorder fundamentally alters the stationary state, producing exponential tails decorated by algebraic prefactors with non-trivial exponents.

</details>


### [115] [Phase Diagram and Critical Behaviour of the Two-Dimensional Potts Model with Long-Range Quenched Disorder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29710)
*Rodolfo Rocha,Leticia F. Cugliandolo,Marco Picco*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究了具有幂律空间相关无序的q=3和q=8 Potts模型的相图与临界性质，通过Fortuin-Kasteleyn簇的环绕概率细化了Chippari等人的相图，并用磁学可观测量澄清了磁化率双峰结构的物理起源，验证了在所有研究范围内超标度关系的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在具有空间相关无序（幂律衰减指数a）的Potts模型中，相图从有限无序不动点向无限无序不动点的转变机制尚不清晰，磁化率的双峰结构物理起源未明，需要更精确的方法来细化和验证已有相图。

Method: 采用Fortuin-Kasteleyn簇的环绕概率来精确表征相图，并结合磁学可观测量（磁化率等）分析双峰结构，检验超尺度关系。

Result: 细化了Chippari等人提出的相图框架，明确了磁化率双峰结构的物理起源，并在所有研究的参数范围内验证了超尺度关系的成立。

Conclusion: 通过环绕概率和磁学观测量的联合分析，深化了对空间相关无序Potts模型临界行为的理解，为有限无序至无限无序不动点转变提供了更精确的物理图像。

Abstract: We study the phase diagram and critical properties of the $q = 3$ and $q = 8$ Potts models with spatially correlated disorder governed by a power-law decay with exponent $a$. Building on the phase diagram proposed by Chippari et al. [3], where a transition from finite-disorder to infinite-disorder fixed points was identified as a function of a, we refine this picture by using wrapping probabilities of Fortuin-Kasteleyn clusters. Furthermore, using magnetic observables, we clarify the physical origin of the double-peak structure in the magnetic susceptibility and establish the validity of the hyper-scaling relation across all investigated regimes.

</details>


### [116] [Representation Transitions Reveal Predictive Structure in Complex Systems: A Trajectory-Level Reconstruction in a Critical System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29777)
*Jiaqi Pan*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文通过在同一临界复杂系统轨迹集合上进行系统性的表征形式搜索，证明预测失败的决定性因素不是相关性强度，而是表征是否保留了与预测相关的结构。连续低维压缩表征在八次独立尺度去除折交叉中稳健地预测成功（r=0.677-0.717，p<0.001），而离散多特征分类尽管其最强特征相关性更高（r=0.588），却无法分离最关键的两个案例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂系统中的轨迹结构往往无法被传统的总体平均标量统计所捕获，但现有研究缺乏对表征形式选择的系统性比较。作者希望通过在同一天真轨迹集合上对比从标量统计到连续低维压缩等多种表征形式，揭示表征选择本身作为影响预测可达性的科学变量的重要性。

Method: 作者摒弃文献综述方式，通过独立数值重模拟，在同一个临界复杂系统轨迹集合上系统性地搜索多种表征形式：从单一标量统计，到两个独立的离散多特征分类方法，再到轨迹级结构表征和诊断性替代测试，最终到连续低维压缩表征。所有表征均在相同的轨迹集合上评估其预测性能。

Result: 离散表征的最强个体特征与目标的相关性（r=0.588）高于成功表征的标志性统计量（r=0.540），但前者无法分离集合中两个最关键的案例；而连续表征在八次独立尺度去除折中稳健地成功（r=0.677-0.717，全部p<0.001）。预测差异的决定因素是表征是否保留了与预测相关的结构，而非相关性强度。

Conclusion: 表征形式的选择决定了复杂系统中预测结构是否变为可及，这本身就是一个科学变量，而非下游分析中的附属选择。研究者应重视表征选择对预测可达性的根本性影响。

Abstract: Trajectories in complex systems often contain structure that is not captured by the population-averaged, scalar statistics traditionally used to describe them. Here we reconstruct, through independent numerical re-simulation rather than literature review, a systematic search across representational forms - from single scalar statistics, through two independent discrete multi-feature classification attempts, to a trajectory-level structural representation and a diagnostic surrogate test, to a continuous low-dimensional compression - applied to the same underlying trajectory ensemble in a critical complex system. Predictive failure is not explained by insufficient correlation: one discrete representation's own strongest individual feature correlates with the target more strongly ($r=0.588$) than the eventual successful representation's own headline statistic ($r=0.540$), yet fails to separate the ensemble's two most consequential cases - while the continuous representation succeeds, robustly, across eight independent scale-removal folds ($r=0.677$-$0.717$, all $p<0.001$). The determining factor was not correlation strength but whether the representation preserved the structure relevant to prediction. These results indicate that the choice of representation determines whether predictive structure in complex systems becomes accessible - a scientific variable in its own right, not a downstream analysis choice.

</details>


### [117] [Branching stochastic mechanics. I. Clustering and connected correlations within a branching-process representation of the Schrödinger equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29807)
*Eric Dumonteil,Benoît Bischoff,Alain Letourneau,Loïc Thulliez,Corentin Doutre*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文通过Schrödinger-Nagasawa变换将波函数表示为正向前向与后向扩散场的乘积，并将其提升为分支超过程。通过Bohm/Fisher控制的随机分支生成谱系学，其均值精确再现Schrödinger动力学。在允许分支率响应于涨落乘积Φ_FΦ_B后，定义了符号核C_FB，其对角项给出正配对密度，非对角项定义关联长度ξ_FB=√(D_eff/μ_FB)。核心发现是扩展的Born密度与有限关联范围可以在同一随机核中共存，暗示粒子状组织。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者质疑在扩展量子态统计中能否隐藏有限范围的关联。传统量子力学框架中波函数给出扩展的Born密度，但统计层次上的有限关联长度（即粒子状结构）能否在扩展态中隐藏是一个根本问题。论文旨在通过随机过程构造一个能同时容纳扩展Born密度和有限关联范围的统计框架，从而弥合量子力学中波函数扩展性与粒子性之间的张力。

Method: 论文采用三步构造：(1) 将Schrödinger-Nagasawa变换中的正扩散场提升为分支超过程Φ_F和Φ_B，分支率由Bohm/Fisher控制，均值精确复现Schrödinger动力学；(2) 分析连通部分的聚类行为（超临界、临界、次临界），确定自由空间的临界维度d_c=2，并展示次临界聚簇获得约化de Broglie尺度；(3) 让分支率响应当地的涨落乘积Φ_FΦ_B，在倒易基中将场分解为光滑参考R加中心化涨落ψ_F,ψ_B，定义符号核C_FB(x,y)=E_ω[ψ_F(x)ψ_B(y)]。通过对关联核的对角项（配对密度）与非对角项（关联长度）分析，结合对/相对坐标的谱分解和谱抵消条件，选取Born集体模与反关联源通道，得到屏蔽长度ξ_FB=√(D_eff/μ_FB)。

Result: 论文证明：Bohm/Fisher控制的分支超过程均值能精确再现Schrödinger动力学；随机连通部分在约束下表现出三种聚类相，自由空间临界维度为d_c=2；次临界聚簇具有约化de Broglie尺度；在反馈分支机制下，明确构造出符号核C_FB，其对角给出正配对密度ρ_BSM(x)=-C_FB(x,x)，在稳态恢复时对角与Born密度匹配，而非对角衰减决定关联范围。最终得到有限屏蔽长度ξ_FB=√(D_eff/μ_FB)，从而证明了扩展Born密度与有限关联范围在同一随机核中的共存可能性。

Conclusion: 本文表明有限范围关联确实可以隐藏在扩展量子态的统计中，这一结论通过分支超过程与反馈机制在数学上得到严格构造。扩展Born密度与有限关联长度在同一随机核中的共存暗示了粒子状组织的新思维方式，为量子-经典边界、测量问题的随机解释以及量子流体中的准粒子描述提供了新的统计基础。该方法将量子动力学、随机过程与聚类统计统一在一个框架中，具有深远的理论意义。

Abstract: Can finite-range correlations hide in the statistics of an extended quantum state? The Schrödinger-Nagasawa transform represents the wave function by positive forward and backward diffusion fields whose product is the Born density. We promote them to branching superprocesses, \(Φ_F\) and \(Φ_B\): diffusion samples stochastic paths, whereas Bohm/Fisher-controlled branching generates genealogies of alternative continuations. With rates evaluated on the prescribed Born density, their means reproduce Schrödinger dynamics exactly. The connected sector exhibits supercritical, critical, and subcritical clustering in confinement and has critical dimension \(d_c=2\) in free space. Stationary eigenmodes remain extended; subcritical clusters acquire the reduced de~Broglie scale. We then let the branching rate respond to the fluctuating product \(Φ_FΦ_B\). In the reciprocal basis, the fields equal a smooth reference \(R\) plus centered fluctuations \(ψ_F,ψ_B\), defining the signed kernel \(C_{\rm FB}(x,y)=\mathbb E_ω[ψ_F(x)ψ_B(y)]\). On the anticorrelated branch, \(ρ_{\rm BSM}(x)=-C_{\rm FB}(x,x)\) is the positive paired density. Stationary recovery requires its diagonal to match the Born profile, while off-diagonal decay defines the correlation range. The pair equation splits into collective and relative sectors: spectral cancellation selects the Born collective mode, while suppression of the leading density fluctuation selects the anticorrelated source channel. With relative diffusivity \(D_{\rm eff}\) and positive relaxation rate \(μ_{\rm FB}\), correlations have screening length \(ξ_{\rm FB}=\sqrt{D_{\rm eff}/μ_{\rm FB}}\). Thus an extended Born density and a finite correlation range can coexist in one stochastic kernel, suggesting particle-like organization.

</details>


### [118] [Asymmetry-controlled resonant transport in a Brownian flashing ratchet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.29991)
*Sudipta Mandal,Dipanjan Chakraborty,Debasish Chaudhuri*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究一维布朗闪烁棘轮中的定向输运现象。通过求解福克-普朗克方程和布朗动力学模拟，发现稳态电流随开关频率呈非单调依赖，存在共振极大值。当势垒不对称度|δ|<0.5时，电流满足标度形式，通过适当缩放可实现数据塌缩。共振频率遵循ν(δ,Δ)=ν₀(Δ)/[1-b₀δ²]的(1-δ²)⁻¹标度规律，并可从非对称势两分支的耦合弛豫角度加以物理解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 闪烁棘轮是研究布朗马达和定向输运的重要理论模型，但其电流对开关频率的依赖性、势垒不对称度与势垒高度对输运特性的影响尚未被充分理解。本文旨在系统研究分段线性非对称周期势下布朗闪烁棘轮的输运行为，揭示电流-频率关系的普适标度规律及其物理机制。

Method: 采用两种互补的数值方法：(1) 数值求解福克-普朗克方程以获得稳态电流的精确解；(2) 布朗动力学模拟作为独立验证。系统扫描开关频率、势垒不对称度δ和势垒高度Δ三个参数空间，并通过电流与频率的适当标度变换来检验数据塌缩。利用分段线性非对称势的两分支结构，建立耦合弛豫的解析框架来解释共振频率的标度行为。

Result: 研究发现：稳态电流随开关频率呈非单调的共振行为，共振峰位置依赖于δ和Δ。在中等不对称度范围(|δ|<0.5)内，电流-频率曲线满足标度形式，电流与频率经适当缩放后可实现数据塌缩。电流幅度随δ线性增加，而共振频率满足ν(δ,Δ)=ν₀(Δ)/[1-b₀δ²]的形式，即(1-δ²)⁻¹标度关系。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了闪烁棘轮中共振频率与非对称性的明确解析关系，并通过非对称势两分支的耦合弛豫动力学提供了物理图像。这种标度普适性表明电流与势参数、频率的依赖可以分离，为理解布朗马达的输运优化和控制提供了理论依据与设计准则。

Abstract: We investigate directed transport in a one-dimensional Brownian flashing ratchet with a piecewise-linear asymmetric periodic potential. Numerical solutions of the Fokker--Planck equation and Brownian dynamics simulations reveal a nonmonotonic dependence of the stationary current on the switching frequency, with a resonant maximum whose position depends on the potential asymmetry $δ$ and barrier height $Δ$. For moderate asymmetry, $|δ|<0.5$, the current obeys a scaling form that separates the dependence on the potential parameters from a common frequency dependence, resulting in a data collapse upon appropriate scaling of the current and frequency. The current amplitude varies linearly with $δ$, while the resonance frequency follows $ν(δ,Δ)=ν_0(Δ)/ [1-b_0\, δ^2]$. We interpret the resulting $(1-δ^2)^{-1}$ scaling in terms of coupled relaxation along the two branches of the asymmetric potential, which provides a physical basis for the observed dependence of the resonant frequency on the potential asymmetry.

</details>


### [119] [Chemical potentials from structure factors: II. Charged multi-component mixtures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30060)
*Xiaoyu Wang,Roya Savoj,Musahid Ahmed,Bingqing Cheng*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该论文将S0方法扩展至带电多组分混合物（电解质体系），通过将中性组分涨落与宏观电荷涨落分离，并考虑电荷中性约束，从平衡分子动力学模拟中高效计算离子混合物的化学势。方法成功应用于多卤化物盐水溶液和熔盐混合物的混合自由能计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 带电多组分混合物的化学势是电解质热力学的核心量，但从原子模拟中计算该量一直困难重重。现有方法在带电体系中面临宏观电荷涨落被禁止、长程静电关联难以处理等挑战，需要一种能够在带电混合物中可靠计算化学势的新方法。

Method: 将第一部分（中性多组分混合物）建立的组分空间框架与带电体系的库仑处理相结合，通过处理静态结构因子在小波数极限下的行为，将热力学相关的中性组分涨落与被禁止的宏观电荷涨落分离，并显式计入电荷中性约束。具体应用包括多卤化物水盐溶液的化学势计算和熔盐混合物的混合自由能计算。

Result: 成功计算了多卤化物水盐溶液中各离子的化学势，揭示了离子特异性热力学效应；同时计算了熔盐混合物的混合自由能，展示了正确描述长波静电相关性对混合自由能计算的重要性。

Conclusion: 该扩展方法为从原子模拟中计算带电多组分混合物（包括电解质溶液和熔盐）的化学势提供了有效的理论框架，强调了长波长静电关联在带电体系热力学计算中的关键作用。

Abstract: The chemical potentials of charged multi-component mixtures are central to electrolyte thermo- dynamics, but remain difficult to compute from atomistic simulations. The S0 method enables computing chemical potentials of mixtures from equilibrium molecular dynamics simulations. Here we extend the S0 method to charged mixtures by combining the composition-space framework developed in Part I: Neutral Multi-component Mixtures with a Coulombic treatment of the small- wavenumber limits of static structure factors. This approach separates thermodynamically relevant neutral composition fluctuations from forbidden macroscopic charge fluctuations, and further ac- counts for charge-neutrality constraints. We use the method to compute the chemical potentials of multiple-halide aqueous salt solutions, elucidating the ion-specific thermodynamic effects. We also calculate the mixing free energies of molten salt mixtures, demonstrating the importance of correctly describing long-wavelength electrostatic correlations.

</details>


### [120] [d'Q = TdS for Infinitesimal Irreversible Processes: On the Differential Form of the Clausius Inequality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30421)
*Hiroshi Shimahara*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文表明，对于无限小唯静态不可逆过程，Clausius不等式的正确一阶形式应为等式 d'Q=TdS 而非 d'Q<TdS，从而证明内熵产生是二阶或更高阶小量，熵在包含唯静态不可逆过程时仍是良定义的状态量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上，Clausius不等式的微分形式 d'Q<TdS 被普遍用于无限小不可逆过程，但在唯静态（准静态）不可逆过程的语境下，这一不等式的正确形式存在争议。作者试图澄清这一点，并给出一个纯粹热力学层面的证明。

Method: 采用纯热力学论证，不借助Boltzmann关系，分析无限小唯静态不可逆过程中Clausius不等式的正确一阶展开形式，并通过热力学推理证明内熵产生项在二阶及以上量级。

Result: 证明无限小唯静态不可逆过程的正确一阶形式是 d'Q=TdS（等式）而非 d'Q<TdS，内熵产生为二阶或更高阶小量，因此熵在包含此类过程时依然是良定义的状态函数。

Conclusion: 该工作纠正了Clausius不等式在无限小唯静态不可逆过程中的表述，表明不等式仅在一阶近似下退化为等式，熵的状态函数性质不依赖Boltzmann关系也能在纯热力学框架内得到确认。

Abstract: Conventionally, the differential form of the Clausius inequality is adopted for infinitesimal irreversible processes. We show that the correct first-order form for infinitesimal quasi-static irreversible processes is the equality d'Q=TdS rather than d'Q < TdS. This provides a purely thermodynamic proof that internal entropy production is second-order or higher and confirms that entropy remains a well-defined state quantity even when quasi-static irreversible processes are included, without resorting to the Boltzmann relation.

</details>


### [121] [Time-delayed feedback turns Arrhenius escape logarithmic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30624)
*Roy Podgaetsky,Vishwajeet Kumar Arnab Pal,Ohad Shpielberg*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文发现由时间延迟反馈引入的非马尔可夫性可以消除热逃逸的阿伦尼乌斯指数标度。当延迟超过由势阱最小值曲率决定的阈值时，势阱失稳，热噪声引发不稳定性后被确定性放大至边界，形成"弹弓"逃逸轨迹。逃逸时间随势垒呈对数标度，其波动服从Gumbel分布，且存在最优延迟使逃逸快于自由扩散。时间延迟由此成为一种可调谐、实验可行的加速激活过程的控制参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统热逃逸遵循阿伦尼乌斯定律，逃逸时间随势垒高度呈指数增长，这限制了激活过程的效率。作者希望寻找一种能够打破这种指数标度的机制，从而加速激活过程。

Method: 通过在束缚力中引入时间延迟反馈来引入非马尔可夫性，研究延迟对势阱稳定性的影响。理论分析揭示了延迟失稳阈值、噪声引发的失稳增长机制（弹弓逃逸），并推导了逃逸时间的标度律及其统计分布。

Result: 延迟超过由最小曲率决定的阈值后，逃逸时间从指数标度转变为对数标度；逃逸时间波动服从Gumbel定律；存在最优延迟可使逃逸快于自由扩散。

Conclusion: 时间延迟可作为可调谐且实验上可行的控制参数，用于加速激活过程，为设计更高效的逃逸和反应动力学方案提供了新途径。

Abstract: Thermal escape is governed by the Arrhenius law, where the mean escape time scales exponentially with the barrier height. We show that the non-Markovianity induced by time-delayed feedback in the confining force removes this exponential scaling. Beyond a threshold set by the curvature of the minimum, the delay destabilizes the well, and the thermal noise seeds an instability that is subsequently amplified deterministically to the boundary leading to \textit{slingshot} escape trajectories. The escape time becomes logarithmic in the barrier, its fluctuations follow a Gumbel law, and an optimal delay enables escape faster than free diffusion. Our results propose time delay as a tunable and experimentally feasible control parameter for accelerating activated processes.

</details>


### [122] [Effective Hardcore Exclusion Without Exclusion: Two-Species Pair Annihilation Revisited](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30630)
*Su-Chan Park,Foster Thompson*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个不含硬核排斥的粒子模型，通过非线性密度依赖的非对称扩散重现了此前仅在硬核排斥系统中观察到的A+B→∅反应扩散体系中t^{-1/3}的密度衰减标度律。研究表明，对湮灭密度衰减指数并非仅由孤立粒子物种的输运性质决定，两体微观非对称过程产生t^{-1/3}，而三体过程则产生与对称扩散相关的t^{-1/4}标度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前已知在硬核排斥系统中，A+B→∅相互湮灭的双物种反应扩散体系在低维下表现出反常幂律衰减，其中对称扩散下ρ~t^{-1/4}，非对称扩散下ρ~t^{-1/3}；但t^{-1/3}行为迄今仅在硬核排斥系统中观察到。作者希望探究这种标度行为是否本质上依赖于硬核排斥，并理解决定密度衰减指数的根本机制。

Method: 构造一个不含硬核排斥的粒子模型，其中两种物种均经历速率随局域密度非线性变化的非对称扩散。该输运形式在无其他物种存在时表现为超扩散，属于Kardar-Parisi-Zhang（KPZ）普适类。通过调节微观过程：涉及粒子对的非对称过程导致t^{-1/3}标度，而涉及三粒子的非对称过程则导致t^{-1/4}标度。

Result: 成功构造出无硬核排斥但能复现t^{-1/3}标度的模型。发现：当至少一种物种经历由粒子对微观过程诱导的非对称扩散时，密度衰减为ρ~t^{-1/3}；而当非对称过程涉及三粒子组时，则恢复为ρ~t^{-1/4}标度（与对称扩散相同）。这证明t^{-1/3}并非硬核排斥的专属特征，且密度衰减指数可由微观非对称过程的阶数而非简单的输运普适类决定。

Conclusion: 该工作证明A+B→∅反应扩散体系中密度衰减的标度指数并非仅由孤立物种的输运性质（如对称/非对称扩散及其普适类）唯一决定。微观非对称过程的阶数（两体vs三体）对最终标度行为起关键作用，深化了对低维反应扩散体系反常动力学的理解。

Abstract: Reaction-diffusion systems with two species that mutually annihilate through $A+B\to\emptyset$ reactions display a slow decay of the total density $ρ$ which follows an anomalous power law in low dimensions. For hardcore particles in one dimension, this decay follows $ρ\sim t^{-1/4}$ under symmetric diffusion and $ρ\sim t^{-1/3}$ under asymmetric diffusion without relative bias between the two species. The $t^{-1/3}$ behavior, in particular, has so far been observed exclusively in systems with hardcore exclusion. Here we construct a model of particles without hardcore exclusion that reproduces this same $t^{-1/3}$ scaling. In our model, both species undergo asymmetric diffusion with a rate that depends nonlinearly on the local density, a form of transport that, in the absence of the other species, is superdiffusive and falls into the Kardar-Parisi-Zhang universality class. The scaling behavior of $t^{-1/3}$ occurs when at least one of the two species undergoes asymmetric diffusion induced by microscopic processes involving pairs of particles, while asymmetric processes involving groups of three particles lead to $t^{-1/4}$ scaling associated with symmetric diffusion. This demonstrates that the pair annihilation density decay exponent is not determined exclusively by the transport properties of the isolated particle species.

</details>


### [123] [Kinetic temperatures and inertial effects in a nonequilibrium bead-spring model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30809)
*Jetin E Thomas,Ramandeep S. Johal*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究了一个由两个任意质量耦合颗粒组成的非平衡稳态模型，该系统与两个不同温度的浴槽接触。通过协方差矩阵方法和欠阻尼Langevin动力学的数值模拟，作者发现不可逆性度量（熵产生、热流）对质量交换保持不变，而能量观测依赖于质量排列。关键发现是动力学温度（kinetic temperatures）是支配系统热力学的自然变量，它们统一定义了能量、输运和不可逆性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在揭示耦合系统中质量分布如何影响非平衡热力学性质，特别是区分哪些可观测量对质量排列敏感（如能量存储），哪些不敏感（如输运和熵产生），并探索一个统一的组织变量来理解系统在奇异质量极限下的热力学行为。

Method: 采用协方差矩阵方法（covariance-matrix approach）对任意质量的两个耦合颗粒系统进行解析分析，并结合数值模拟求解欠阻尼Langevin动力学。系统与两个不同温度的浴槽接触，作者表征了稳态概率分布、热输运和熵产生，并分析了动能温度作为组织变量的作用。

Result: 发现熵产生和热流对质量交换不变，而能量存储依赖于质量排列。动力学温度的差值控制输运和熵产生，其和通过广义均分关系决定平均能量。在无限质量极限下，只有以动力学温度表达的构成关系仍然有意义。推导出了定义平衡类正则分布的有效温度，并显示时间平均观测量比系综平均收敛快得多。

Conclusion: 能量、输运和不可逆性通过动力学温度作为组织变量被统一起来。这项研究揭示了能量观测在奇异质量极限下具有路径依赖性，而输运和不可逆性保持良好定义，为理解非平衡耗散系统中的质量排列效应提供了新的理论框架。

Abstract: We investigate a nonequilibrium steady-state model consisting of two coupled beads with arbitrary masses in contact with two thermal baths at different temperatures. Using a covariance-matrix approach together with numerical simulations of the underdamped Langevin dynamics, we characterize steady-state probability distributions, heat transport, and entropy production. We show that irreversibility measures such as entropy production and heat current are invariant under an exchange of the bead masses, whereas energy-storage observables depend explicitly on the mass arrangement in a symmetrical set up. This reveals a fundamental distinction: energy observables exhibit path dependence in singular mass limits, while transport and irreversibility remain well defined. We show that kinetic temperatures provide the natural variables governing the thermodynamics of the system: their difference controls transport and entropy production, while their sum determines the mean energy via a model specific generalized equipartition relation. In the infinite-mass limit, only constitutive relations expressed in terms of kinetic temperatures remain meaningful. Thus, energy, transport, and irreversibility are unified through kinetic temperatures as the organizing variables. We also derive an effective temperature that defines an equilibrium-like canonical distribution. Finally, we analyze the notion of ergodicity and show that the time-averaged observables converge significantly faster than the ensemble averages.

</details>


### [124] [Inertia-Driven Information Flow and Symmetry Breaking in a Nonequilibrium Two-Bead System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30849)
*Jetin E. Thomas,Ramandeep S. Johal*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究了一个耦合到两个热浴的非平衡双珠系统中的信息流产生机制，发现在过阻尼和欠阻尼两种情况下系统均可作为信息流发生器，并揭示了欠阻尼动力学中在热不对称-惯性参数空间中沿特定路径出现的信息流发散现象，该现象在过阻尼极限下被隐藏。对称性微扰分析揭示了信息流响应景观中类似金兹堡-朗道框架的几何结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索非平衡纳米机械系统中信息流的产生机制，为构建信息引擎和类妖机制提供理论途径，并理解惯性与微观异质性如何塑造非平衡系统中的信息景观。

Method: 采用耦合到两个不同温度热浴的双珠模型，分别在过阻尼和欠阻尼动力学框架下进行分析；通过对称性微扰分析研究信息流响应景观，结合Hessian特征值和特征向量分析揭示几何结构；在热不对称-惯性参数空间中考察标度信息流的发散行为。

Result: 系统在过阻尼和欠阻尼两种机制下均能产生信息流；欠阻尼下沿特定参数路径出现标度信息流发散，该效应在过阻尼极限下被隐藏；对称参考态可对应信息流景观的最小值或最大值（取决于扰动方向）；惯性效应使扁平化的过阻尼景观产生有限曲率；质量不对称使最大值发生偏移；Hessian分析揭示主模式能级交叉及沿恒定扩散路径的双峰性。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了理解惯性和微观异质性如何塑造非平衡系统信息景观的最小化框架，为信息引擎设计提供了理论依据，并有望扩展到更复杂的异质网络系统。

Abstract: We investigate information-flow generation in a nonequilibrium two-bead system coupled to two heat baths. We show that the system acts as an information-flow generator in both overdamped and underdamped regimes, with the underdamped dynamics revealing a divergence of the scaled information flow along specific paths in the thermal asymmetry--inertia parameter space that is hidden in the overdamped limit. This information generation suggests a possible route toward information engines and demon-like mechanisms in nanomachines. A symmetry-perturbation analysis of the response landscape of information flow reveals a geometric structure reminiscent of a Ginzburg--Landau framework: the symmetric reference state can correspond to a minimum or maximum depending on the perturbation direction, while the flat overdamped landscape develops a finite curvature under inertia. Mass asymmetry shifts the resulting maxima, and Hessian eigenvalue and eigenvector analysis reveals level touching of principal modes and bimodality along a constant-diffusion path. These results establish a minimal framework for understanding how inertia and microscopic heterogeneity shape information landscapes in nonequilibrium systems, with potential extensions to more complex heterogeneous networks.

</details>


### [125] [On the relaxation problem in statistical mechanics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30871)
*Giuseppe Del Vecchio Del Vecchio*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文将统计力学中的弛豫问题重新表述为运行对象（即记录的信号Z(t)的局部时间统计）的弛豫，这些统计量等价于无时钟观察者在均匀随机时间采样得到的观测直方图。预测对象是对未来新鲜样本读出结果的信念，该分布由被认为正确的数学模型推断。对于有限有界N≥1自由度系统，全局不可逆弛豫可能发生但存在特殊初始条件。预测形式取决于观察者选择的损失函数。熵被赋予学习解释——即观察者与系统未知过去之间的互信息，其平稳值完全普遍地依赖于可用信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者试图重新审视统计力学中弛豫问题的根本基础，指出传统的弛豫对象（如微观态或宏观变量）在操作层面上不够清晰，需要明确界定什么才是真正经历弛豫的运行性对象。通过引入"无时钟观察者"和"局部时间统计"的概念，作者希望为不可逆弛豫、熵和预测问题提供一个更根本、更普遍的操作性框架，摆脱对时间箭头或特定系统动力学的先验假设。

Method: 作者对统计力学中的弛豫问题进行了概念重构：（1）将弛豫的载体定义为运行对象——观测信号Z(t)的局部时间统计（等价于均匀随机时刻采样的直方图）；（2）将预测定义为对未来新鲜样本读数的信念，其分布由假设的数学模型推断；（3）考察有限有界N≥1系统下的可逆与不可逆弛豫条件；（4）引入损失函数的概念以刻画不同观察者预测形式的选择自由度；（5）将熵重新解释为观察者与系统未知过去之间的互信息，并分析其平稳值与可用信息量的关系。

Result: 论文得出以下结果：（1）即使对于有限有界的N≥1自由度系统，全局不可逆的预测弛豫也可以发生，但需要存在特殊初始条件；（2）预测的具体形式依赖于观察者所选定的损失函数，具有主体依赖性；（3）熵获得了一种学习论解释——它是观察者与系统未知过去的互信息，其平稳值在完全普遍的情况下取决于观察者可获得的信息量，而非仅由系统本身决定。

Conclusion: 该文表明统计力学中的弛豫、预测和熵这三者都应从观察者的操作性视角重新理解：弛豫的对象是局部时间统计，预测的形式依损失函数而变，熵的本质是观察者的信息获取量。不可逆性并非有限系统的绝对属性，而是与初始条件和观察者信息状态相关联的相对属性。

Abstract: We reformulate the relaxation problem in statistical mechanics by making explicit what are the \emph{operational} objects subject to relaxation: the local time statistics of the recorded signal $Z(t)$. These local time statistics are simply the estimated histograms of observations $\{Z(t_i)\}_{i=1}^M$ performed at uniformly random times $\{t_i\}_{i=1}^M$ by a clockless observer. The subject of prediction is a belief about a future fresh out-of-sample reading of a measurement outcome whose distribution is inferred from the mathematical model believed to be true. For finite bounded systems of $N\ge 1$ degrees of freedom global irreversible relaxation of predictions can occur but special initial conditions exist. The form of the predictions depends on certain loss functions whose choice is up to the particular observer. Finally, entropy is given a learning interpretation as mutual information between the observer and the unknown past of the system under consideration and, in complete generality, its stationary value depends on the information available.

</details>


### [126] [Compensation in continuous symmetric trilayered planar ferrimagnet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.30941)
*Olivia Mallick,Muktish Acharyya*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文通过蒙特卡洛模拟研究了三层（A-B-A型）各向异性单点XY磁性模型，该模型具有层内铁磁和层间反铁磁相互作用。研究发现该系统在特定参数范围内表现出高温临界行为和低温补偿行为，且补偿温度和临界温度均随单点各向异性的增强而增大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究三层各向异性XY磁性模型（A-B-A型）的平衡性质，特别是探索该系统在层内铁磁和层间反铁磁相互作用下的临界行为和补偿行为，以及单点各向异性对这两种温度的影响。

Method: 采用蒙特卡洛模拟方法，使用Metropolis单自旋（随机选择）更新算法。研究层内铁磁与层间反铁磁相互作用的三层各向异性XY模型，绘制综合相图，并通过有限尺寸研究分析临界温度附近的关联增长。

Result: 在特定相互作用参数范围内，系统展现出高温临界行为（亚晶格磁化强度和总磁化强度均消失）和低温补偿行为（总磁化强度消失但亚晶格磁化强度非零）。补偿温度和临界温度均随单点各向异性的增加而升高。有限尺寸研究表明临界温度附近存在关联长度的增长。

Conclusion: 三层各向异性XY磁模型能够同时表现出低温补偿和高温临界两种行为，单点各向异性对两种温度均有增强作用，完整的相图已建立，有限尺寸分析证实了临界温度附近关联的扩展。

Abstract: The trilayered (A-B-A type) anisotropic (single site) XY magnetic model is considered with the intra-plane ferromagnetic and
  the inter-plane antiferromagnetic interactions. The equilibrium properties of such trilayered anisotropic XY system has been studied by
  Monte Carlo simulation with Metropolis single spin (randomly chosen) update algorithm. In a given range of interaction parameters, the system has been found to exhibit the high temperature {\it critical} (vanishing sublattice magnetisations and total magnetisation) and low temperature {\it compensation} (vanishing total magnetisation even for non-vanishing sublattice magnetisations) behaviours. The compensation temperature and the critical temperature both are found to increase with the increase of single site anisotropy. The comprehensive phase diagram is drawn. The finite size study reveals the growth of correlations near the critical temperature.

</details>


### [127] [Overcoming critical slowing down in frustrated spin systems by learned multiscale sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.31114)
*Gabriele Bandini,Giulio Biroli,Patrick Charbonneau,Andrea Gambassi*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文提出使用小波条件重正化群（WCRG）采样方法来学习而非构造簇，从而克服受阻二维软自旋模型中临界减速问题。该方法通过从粗到细尺度递归采样条件小波分布来生成构型，在Ising类临界点处整体采样复杂度为O(log2 L)，远优于标准局部MCMC方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的簇算法（如Swendsen-Wang和Wolff方法）是缓解临界减速最成功的MCMC方法，但在存在即使是极弱受阻（frustration）的情况下会失效。这一根本性限制促使研究者探索替代方案——通过学习而非构造相关簇来生成构型。

Method: 利用小波条件重正化群（WCRG）采样方法学习受阻二维软自旋模型中集体涨落的概率分布。该方法在从粗到细的尺度上进行递归采样，每个尺度上通过采样条件小波分布来生成构型，采用基于能量的模型来估计小波条件分布。

Result: WCRG方法能在不同相中复现系统的主要统计性质，包括局域场分布和结构因子。在Ising类临界点处，每个尺度的条件动力学在O(1)次扫描内去相关，总体采样复杂度为O(log2 L)，远优于标准局部MCMC方法。

Conclusion: 学习型多尺度采样能够克服传统簇算法无法处理的受阻系统中的临界减速问题。WCRG方法的主要权衡在于：快速采样方案的精度取决于用于估计小波条件分布的能量基模型的表达能力。

Abstract: Cluster algorithms, such as the Swendsen--Wang and Wolff methods, are among the most successful MCMC methods for mitigating critical slowing down in statistical systems. These constructive cluster algorithms, however, fail in the presence of even extremely weak frustration. Here, we sidestep this fundamental limitation by learning rather than constructing the relevant clusters. Specifically, we use the wavelet conditional renormalization group (WCRG) sampling method to learn the probability distribution of collective fluctuations of a frustrated two-dimensional soft-spin model. Configurations are then generated recursively from coarse to fine scales by sampling conditional wavelet distributions. The WCRG method reproduces the main statistical properties of the system across different phases, including the local-field distribution and the structure factor. At an Ising-like critical point, the conditional dynamics remains decorrelated within $\mathcal{O}(1)$ sweeps at each scale, yielding an overall sampling complexity of $\mathcal{O}(\log_2 L)$, thus making WCRG much more efficient than standard local MCMC methods. These results show that learned multiscale sampling can overcome critical slowing down in frustrated systems for which conventional cluster algorithms fail. By assessing the sampling accuracy of different observables, we also clarify the main tradeoff of the WCRG method: the accuracy of the fast sampling scheme depends on the expressiveness of the energy-based model used to estimate the wavelet conditional distributions.

</details>
